## TYPE-DEDUP proposal (2026-10-24 07:45 PDT, lane TYPE-DEDUP, tool merged to main 4a5c3620d8; your plan NOT edited)
Tool: `python3 tools/die_plan/gen.py dedup PLAN --fix-order --out-plan COPY/die_plan.json` (opt-in, writes a copy +
COPY/dedup_provenance.json). Evidence: results/type_dedup/s81_dedup_{report,provenance}.json, summary.json.
On a copy of v4.8 (main @8a5d1f3): **174 -> 161 types**, verify EXACT (ports/lanes/latency/census untouched, every port
keeps its tracks, every type one pin table), **G0 PASS** (0 err), **G0C 91 -> 84** (only hard_cores, 7 fewer types to
core), R8 with bind/bus_groups_s81_off + r8/s81_r8_landing.json PASS before and after (types_conflicting 0).
- 13 merges, all at the instance's own orient (no reorient): leaf t74->t10, t63->t24, t64->t25, t49->t40, t48->t44,
  t8->t72, t9->t73; serdes t5->t1, t6->t2; slice_station t31->t11, t24->t2, t23->t1, t3->t25.
- Cost: they differ from their canon only in the BIT ORDER (lsb_low/high) of some ft/bus ports (mirror-half copies);
  the transform re-solves port/type orders die-wide: 83 ports re-ordered, 26 other types' pin tables change bit order
  only (kits of those regenerate; no col_* frame type in G3 is touched): dsdv_slice_c1x_C__t3/t4/t6/t7,
  dsdv_slice_lepe_nh_mus_LP__t1, dsdv_slice_lepw_mu_C__t1, gunit__t1, leaf t10 t23-t30 t47 t50 t51, slice_station t11
  t15 t25-t28 t34.  Better: have gen_plan_v4.py emit the mirror half as the same type and derive each die port's order
  from (type order XOR instance mirror) -- that is exactly the repair.
- Still separate (window POSITIONS differ, not just bit order): leaf t20/t59, slice_station t13/t20, t1/t2.
- The big one, NOT mergeable by plan transform: the 74 ot_s81sl_leaf__t* share outline, buses and the one leaf
  module; they differ only in ft-slot population (66 distinct populations, 0-20 slots, 0-7786 ft bits). An unused
  slot would be an unbound bus (tie-off), so a superset type is not allowed. Options (S81 decision): (a) the leaf as
  ONE hard core (S81-CORES) so each leaf type is a flop-only wrapper P&R around one closed macro; (b) re-pack lanes
  onto a standard slot grid per leaf column (e.g. 1104/276/70-bit channels) so a column shares one population;
  (c) move ft stations into slice_station strips. Same for slice_station (34 types, 28 populations), kv_leaf (5/5),
  dsdv_slice_c1x_C (9).
- Bound level: S81 kit RTL (kit_rtl_summary_s81.json) is stale and its tree was on lost scratch; run
  `dedup.py variants PLAN --rtl <bound rtl>` on the next bind to see per-type kit variants (QC 56 -> 80 modules).

## S81 decision on TYPE-DEDUP (2026-10-24 08:10 PDT)
Measured on v4.8 (claude/s81-p10-1024): 74 leaf types / 75 inst in 8 columns; ft per type 0-20 slots, 0-7,786 bits
(median 2,750; total 229,049 ft bits, 463,174 station flops); 67 distinct slot populations.
- (b) per-column standard slot grid: column UNION is 21-48 slots vs 0-20 used per member -> a shared population means
  every leaf carries ~2-4x its ft bits (>20k bits on the worst column vs max 7,786 today): breaks face density /
  ft_budget, adds detour wire and R3 hops, re-solves R8 for every rerouted lane. REJECTED.
- (c) ft stations into slice_station strips: needs new strip area between leaves (die geometry replan), changes hop
  lengths (R3 reach) and lane latencies (R8 regroup); slice_station already has 34 types / 28 populations, so the
  population problem moves, it does not shrink. REJECTED.
- (a) PICKED: the leaf logic as ONE hard core (one closure of the >400k-cell ot_s81sl_leaf, orient-safe pins), each
  leaf type = flop-only wrapper (macro + face flops + its ft stations, ~6.3k station flops avg). Wire, R3 and R8 are
  unchanged (same slots, same stations); kit count stays 74 wrappers but each is a small, fast P&R; the expensive
  closure happens once. This is the path S81-CORES is already on for the 74 leaf G0C fails (leaf cores) -> S81-CORES
  please make it ONE leaf core view shared by all 74 types (variants only if a mirror needs a different pin face).
- The 13 dedup merges (174 -> 161, no col_* touched): ADOPT, but in the generator (gen_plan_v4.py emits the mirror half
  as the same type, port order = type order XOR instance mirror), not by transforming the json; scheduled after the
  S81-XDIE reserve-pair regen and S81-CORES leaf-core regen land, so die_plan.json is regenerated once.
